into a local API backend for Claude Code, Codex, OpenCode and OpenAI-compatible clients.</strong></p>\n<p dir=\"auto\">OpenAI Chat Completions • OpenAI Responses • Anthropic Messages • Browser Console • Streaming • Tools • Model Routing</p>\n</div>\n<hr>\n<div class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"><h2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\">Why OmniClaw?</h2><a id=\"user-content-why-omniclaw\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Why OmniClaw?\" href=\"#why-omniclaw\"><svg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"><path d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"></path></svg></a></div>\n<p dir=\"auto\">Most AI coding tools speak different API dialects. OmniClaw GLM Proxy makes them speak to the same local AutoClaw backend without forcing you to juggle separate providers, tokens or adapters.</p>\n<p dir=\"auto\">It exposes three compatibility layers from one local setup:</p>\n<markdown-accessiblity-table data-catalyst=\"\"><table>\n<thead>\n<tr>\n<th>Client ecosystem</th>\n<th>API</th>\n<th>Local endpoint</th>\n<th align=\"right\">Default port</th>\n</tr>\n</thead>\n<tbody>\n<tr>\n<td>OpenAI-compatible apps</td>\n<td>Chat Completions</td>\n<td><code>/v1/chat/completions</code></td>\n<td align=\"right\"><code>18791</code></td>\n</tr>\n<tr>\n<td>Codex / Responses clients</td>\n<td>Responses API</td>\n<td><code>/v1/responses</code></td>\n<td align=\"right\"><code>18791</code></td>\n</tr>\n<tr>\n<td>Claude Code / Anthropic SDK</td>\n<td>Messages API</td>\n<td><code>/v1/messages</code></td>\n<td align=\"right\"><code>18792</code></td>\n</tr>\n</tbody>\n</table></markdown-accessiblity-table>\n<p dir=\"auto\">AutoClaw remains the upstream provider and handles authentication through its local session. No official Z.AI API key is required for the normal AutoClaw flow.</p>\n<div align=\"center\" dir=\"auto\">\n  <a target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener noreferrer\" href=\"/guell11/OmniClaw-GLM-Proxy-for-Claude-Code-Codex-OpenCode/blob/main/assets/architecture.svg\"><img src=\"/guell11/OmniClaw-GLM-Proxy-for-Claude-Code-Codex-OpenCode/raw/main/assets/architecture.svg\" alt=\"OmniClaw architecture\" width=\"100%\" style=\"max-width: 100%;\"></a>\n</div>\n<hr>\n<div class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"><h2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\">✨ Highlights</h2><a id=\"user-content--highlights\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: ✨ Highlights\" href=\"#-highlights\"><svg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"><path d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"></path></svg></a></div>\n<ul dir=\"auto\">\n<li><strong>One local bridge, three API formats</strong> — OpenAI Chat Completions, OpenAI Responses and Anthropic Messages.</li>\n<li><strong>Claude Code ready</strong> — points Claude Code at the Anthropic-compatible proxy on port <code>18792</code>.</li>\n<li><strong>Codex ready</strong> — integrates through the OpenAI Responses API on port <code>18791</code>.</li>\n<li><strong>OpenCode ready</strong> — configures an OpenAI-compatible provider without storing the proxy key in its JSON config.</li>\n<li><strong>AutoClaw authentication</strong> — reads AutoClaw's local authentication state while the desktop app is running and logged in.</li>\n<li><strong>Streaming + tool calls</strong> — supports streaming responses, function calls and <code>function_call_output</code> compatibility.</li>\n<li><strong>Long conversation support</strong> — includes <code>/v1/responses/compact</code> for local compacted context handling.</li>\n<li><strong>Runtime model catalog</strong> — model availability comes from AutoClaw's current runtime configuration instead of pretending hard-coded models exist forever. A rare outbreak of common sense.</li>\n<li><strong>Browser console</strong> — local metrics, endpoint copy buttons, recent clients, model distribution, latency, rate limits and integration helpers.</li>\n<li><strong>Local-first configuration</strong> — binds to <code>127.0.0.1</code> by default and uses a configurable local proxy key.</li>\n<li><strong>Optional OpenRouter fallback</strong> — can use <code>z-ai/glm-5.2</code> through OpenRouter when configured.</li>\n</ul>\n<hr>\n<div class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"><h2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" listens only on <code>127.0.0.1</code>.</p>\n<p dir=\"auto\"><code>PROXY_KEY</code> is a local password for this proxy. It is <strong>not</strong> your AutoClaw credential.</p>\n<p dir=\"auto\">The project does not automate the <code>chat.z.ai</code> textarea, read browser cookies, extract browser session tokens or turn the free web interface into an API. browser console can use <code>z-ai/glm-5.2</code> as an automatic fallback for authentication/quota failures.</p>\n<p dir=\"auto\">Without OpenRouter, OmniClaw surfaces the upstream error and can prepare a compact manual handoff for continuing in <code>chat.z.ai</code>.</p>\n<p dir=\"auto\">The handoff is intentionally small and should include only:</p>\n<ul dir=\"auto\">\n<li>current objective;</li>\n<li>important decisions and constraints;</li>\n<li>necessary files or snippets;</li>\n<li>current state and next step.</li>\n</ul>\n<hr>\n<div class=\"markdown-heading\" at a time; multi-account pooling is not supported.</li>\n<li>The proxy watches the local token file so AutoClaw can rotate authentication during a session without requiring a proxy restart.</li>\n<li>Rate limiting is enforced per client IP, with a default of <code>30 req/s</code>.</li>\n<li>An upstream <code>400 invalid request</code> is retried once after a short delay before the error is surfaced.</li>\n<li>The proxy core stays in native Node.js; React and Vite are used only to build the local browser console in <code>public/</code>.</li>\n</ul>\n<hr>\n<div
